During learning, LifeMem clusters accumulated interaction trajectories based on underlying workflows to extract reusable skills. When solving a new task at inference time, the agent recalls relevant skills and trajectories to guide actions. To validate our method, we conduct experiments across 10 environments and over 13k tasks with 2k newly annotated interaction trajectories. Results show that LifeMem enables effective experience reuse in lifelong learning, achieving both reduced forgetting on learned tasks and superior cross-task transfer. Further analysis reveals that task streaming impacts learning, while consolidating structurally similar trajectories within memory boosts performance.

</details>


### [106] [Doc2FRC: Length-Consistent Document-Level Machine Translation via Fixed-Range Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12674)
*Xiaotian Wang,Youyuan Lin,Zhan Shen,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出固定范围分块(FRC)方法，用动态规划将文档分块到预设长度区间，缓解训练-推理长度不匹配，提升文档级机器翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM虽减少截断，但直接Doc2Doc翻译仍易出现n-gram重复和质量退化。常用分块方法但规则化分块无法处理训练与推理的长度分布不匹配问题。

Method: FRC用动态规划将文档划分到预定义长度区间内的分块，训练与推理一致应用以统一长度分布；并设计轻量级双边界匹配算法进行分块对齐，以及四种训练策略。

Result: 基于FRC的微调显著优于直接Doc2Doc微调，在IWSLT2017上超过现有DocMT方法，改善7B LLM效果。同时构建10语种GlobVDoc测试集，验证FRC提升分布外文档翻译。

Conclusion: FRC通过统一训练推理长度分布和分块对齐，有效缓解DocMT质量问题，并在分布外场景下表现良好。

Abstract: Advanced large language models (LLMs) with long context windows can substantially reduce input truncation in document-level machine translation (DocMT). However, direct Doc2Doc translation remains prone to n-gram repetition and progressive quality degradation. A common remedy is to segment the document into finer-grained chunks. Nonetheless, conventional rule-based chunking approaches fail to handle the length distribution mismatch between training and inference. To address this, we introduce Fixed-Range Chunking (FRC), utilizing dynamic programming to partition documents into chunks within a predefined length interval. By consistently applying FRC during training and inference, the input documents of any length are mapped to the same length distribution, substantially reducing train-test length mismatch. Centered on FRC, we propose a lightweight dual-boundary matching algorithm for chunk alignment, alongside four distinct training strategies. Experimental results show that FRC-based fine-tuning substantially improves 7B LLMs over direct Doc2Doc fine-tuning and outperforms existing DocMT methods on IWSLT2017. We further construct GlobVDoc, a 10-language test set independent of mainstream DocMT training sources, and show that FRC improves out-of-distribution document translation.

</details>


### [107] [DuplexDrama: A Synthesized Dialogue Dataset with Scenarios, Full-Duplex Behaviors, Expressive Speech, and Sound Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12872)
*Qingxiang Guo,Wenke Fan,Shuofeng Zhao,Dawei Yang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Hongwei Cai,Zhou Wang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DuplexDrama是首个同时覆盖完整人格/场景、三种全双工行为、人格对齐情感语音和脚本感知音效的合成语音对话数据集，含2000+小时音频，将开放6400段双语对话（800小时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工语音对话数据集缺乏对人格/场景、多种全双工行为、情感表达和音效的统一覆盖，制约全双工对话模型研究。

Method: 通过4阶段流水线构建DuplexDrama；使用64种音色池覆盖13种人格和5个年龄段；对脚本和合成音频进行质量验证，并通过内部全双工模型训练验证数据有效性。

Result: 生成2000+小时音频，3.8%话轮含至少一种全双工行为；将发布6400段双语对话（800小时，中文约500小时+英文约300小时）及LLM评判提示词。

Conclusion: DuplexDrama为全双工语音对话模型研究提供高质量、多维度的大规模数据支持，推动该领域发展。

Abstract: We present DuplexDrama, the first synthesized spoken dialogue dataset that simultaneously covers four dimensions: (i) complete persona and scenario settings; (ii) three full-duplex behaviors (interruption, backchannel, incomplete); (iii) expressive speech with persona-aligned emotion labels; and (iv) script-aware sound events. DuplexDrama is built via a 4-stage pipeline; quality validation on both scripts and synthesized audio confirms its quality. We have produced more than 2,000 hours audio data with a 64-voice timbre pool spanning 13 personas and 5 age buckets; 3.8% of all turns carry at least one full-duplex behavior. This data has been validated through internal full-duplex model training. We will release a curated subset of 6,400 bilingual dialogues (800 h, Chinese ~500 h + English ~300 h) to advance full-duplex spoken dialogue model research. Data samples are available at our demo page and LLM-judge evaluation prompts will be released with the dataset.

</details>


### [108] [MedSNIP: Building and Benchmarking Snippet-Level Granularity for Medical Fact Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12884)
*Hasan Iqbal,Sarfraz Ahmad,Hyunjae Kim,Sihyeon Park,Junjie Liao,Qingyu Chen,Preslav Nakov,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将医学事实核查重新表述为片段级（snippet-level）验证，提出人工标注基准MedSNIP-Bench和自动片段生成管线MedSNIP，保留局部临床结构，在多个数据集上保持或提升假类F1，并将验证器调用减少24-73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学断言的对错常依赖其周围临床结构（如实验室参考范围、因果/条件链接、患者个体信息），原子级拆分会使这些依赖被割裂，导致验证器面对临床不完整的断言。

Method: 提出片段级验证：以子句分组单元保留局部临床结构。构建MedSNIP-Bench人工标注基准（276个消费者健康与临床情景回复，切分为2524个片段，含一般/患者情境双标签与6种结构模式码）；并开发MedSNIP自动片段生成管线，先与人工边界对比评估，再用于外部语料生成片段单元。

Result: 在MedSNIP-Bench、HealthFC、MedHallu上，片段级验证保持或提升假类F1，增益集中在答案足够长易被割裂、验证器足够强能利用恢复结构的情况；最大合并模式增益出现在因果-条件临床链。验证器调用减少24-73%，端到端节省仅在拆分成本低时成立，开权重拆分器可无损保真实现。

Conclusion: 片段级验证通过保留临床结构，在不牺牲性能的前提下提升医学事实核查效率，拆分成本是端到端收益的关键。

Abstract: A medical claim's correctness often depends not on the claim alone, but on the clinical structure around it. A claim may require a lab reference range, a causal or conditional link, or patient-specific details to be judged correctly, and atom-level decomposition can fragment these dependencies, leaving the verifier with clinically incomplete claims. We reformulate medical fact-checking around snippet-level verification, where clause-grouped units preserve local clinical structure. We introduce MedSNIP-Bench, a human-annotated benchmark for snippet-level medical fact verification, and MedSNIP, an automatic snippet-generation pipeline. MedSNIP-Bench covers 276 consumer-health and clinical-vignette responses, segmented into 2,524 snippets with dual in-general and in-patient-context labels and six structural pattern codes. MedSNIP is evaluated against human snippet boundaries on MedSNIP-Bench and then used to generate snippet-level units for external corpora. Across MedSNIP-Bench, HealthFC, and MedHallu, snippet-level verification preserves or improves false-class F1, with gains concentrated where answers are long enough to fragment and where the verifier is strong enough to exploit the recovered structure. The largest merge-pattern gain is on causal-conditional clinical chains. It also reduces verifier calls by 24-73%, though the saving survives end-to-end only when decomposition is cheap, which an open-weight decomposer makes possible at no loss of chunking fidelity.

</details>


### [109] [Parameter-Efficient Retrievers for Polish and European Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12913)
*Sławomir Dadas,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec,Michał Perełkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出三阶段训练流水线，用无标注数据训练紧凑高效的多语言稠密检索器 PolDense 和 EuroDense，性能媲美更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模稠密检索依赖数十亿参数模型，内存与计算成本高，导致索引、更新与低延迟服务代价大。

Method: 三阶段训练：跨语言对齐、关系知识蒸馏、对比微调；无需原始真值相关标签，仅用强嵌入模型与重排器作为教师生成监督。支持最长8192 token。

Result: PolDense 含6个17M–1B参数波兰语检索器；EuroDense 435M支持9种欧洲语言。评测覆盖41个波兰语及150个多语言任务。PolDense-1B 超过评测中至多9B参数的模型，家族构成Pareto前沿；1B以下 EuroDense 任务均值与语言均值均第一，9种语言中7种领先。

Conclusion: 该流水线可实现高质量-高效率权衡的紧凑检索器，模型全部公开。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems increasingly rely on multi-billion-parameter language models, whose memory and computational requirements make large-scale indexing, frequent corpus updates, and low-latency serving costly. We present a three-stage training pipeline for developing compact and efficient retrievers that remain competitive with substantially larger models. The pipeline combines cross-lingual alignment, relational knowledge distillation, and contrastive fine-tuning. It requires no original ground-truth relevance labels, relying exclusively on supervision generated by strong embedding models and rerankers utilised as teachers. Using this pipeline, we develop PolDense and EuroDense, both supporting contexts of up to 8,192 tokens. PolDense is a family of six Polish retrievers ranging from 17M to 1B parameters. EuroDense is a 435M-parameter retriever supporting nine European languages. We conduct an extensive evaluation covering 41 Polish and 150 multilingual retrieval tasks. The results demonstrate strong quality-efficiency trade-offs. PolDense-1B outperforms the evaluated retrievers with up to 9B parameters, while the PolDense family forms the Pareto frontier across model sizes. Among the evaluated models below 1B parameters, EuroDense ranks first in both task-averaged and language-averaged performance and leads in seven of nine languages. We release all models publicly.

</details>


### [110] [LLM-Enhanced Dual-Branch Learning for Large-Scale Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12915)
*Hui Ye,Jing Zhang,Xiulong Yang,Rajshekhar Sunderraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DualMLC双分支框架，用自回归解码器与双向编码器分别估计标签相关性，通过后期logit融合提升大规模多标签文本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表示的方法多以单一主编码器为核心，或在一体化排序器中融合辅助特征，未能充分挖掘异构语言模型之间的互补性。

Method: DualMLC将同一文档分别输入自回归解码器语言模型和双向编码器，两分支各自保留独立表示路径，并在共享标签空间上独立估计相关性分数，最后通过late logit fusion融合两个分数向量。

Result: 在三个常用大规模多标签文本分类基准上达到SOTA；消融实验表明异构预测器融合比任一单分支排名更强。

Conclusion: 异构语言模型的分支融合可让共享证据强化相关标签、分支特有证据互补，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-label text classification assigns a small subset of relevant labels to each document from a vocabulary containing thousands or tens of thousands of candidate labels. Although pretrained language models have improved semantic text representations, most representation-based approaches center their prediction pipelines on a primary encoder or combine auxiliary features within a single ranker. The complementarity between heterogeneous language models therefore remains insufficiently explored. We propose DualMLC, a dual-branch framework that processes the same document through an autoregressive decoder-only language model and a bidirectional encoder. Each branch maintains its own representation pathway and independently estimates relevance scores over the shared label space. DualMLC combines the two score vectors through late logit fusion, allowing shared evidence to reinforce relevant labels and branch-specific evidence to compensate for limitations in the other branch's representation. DualMLC achieves state-of-the-art results on three widely used large-scale multi-label text classification benchmarks. Ablation results further confirm that integrating the heterogeneous predictors produces stronger rankings than either branch alone. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/huiyegit/DualMLC.

</details>


### [111] [Fewer Words, Not Fewer Tokens: Measuring the Sanskrit Tokenization Penalty per Proposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12960)
*Devansh Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在相同内容上，梵语按义素计的token成本是否高于英语取决于分词器与语料领域；与匹配英语对照时差异消失，真正存留的是字符级长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梵语将格、数、人称、时态融合于词尾并以复合词串联子句，单词信息密度高，但这一密度能否在子词分词后保留，需按义素单位而非单词单位重新检验。作者旨在厘清此前"梵语分词更贵/更便宜"结论是否由分词器、词汇表大小与语域混淆所致。

Method: 在FLORES-200 devtest 同一内容上，用部署分词器（词表≥200,019）比较梵语与英语、印地语的token数；训练BPE，规模为32k/64k/128k，设配对匹配与字节匹配对照；在当代散文、梵语诗律与FLORES上测量每义素token比，并将其分解为字符长度比与每字符token比。

Result: 梵语对英语为1.774-2.187倍，对印地语仅1.325-1.353倍；对部署英语分词器，梵语训练BPE在当代散文上为0.887。换用同语料英语侧训练的匹配对照后该反转消失：32k与64k下8个匹配对均高于1.0，95%区间不含1。词汇表增大差距缩小，128k时域内0.983，域外1.025，FLORES 1.116。比值分解后每字符token比近1，存留的是字符长度：散文SLP1为1.028，诗律0.596。部署实况下梵语每义素成本为英语1.831-2.899倍。

Conclusion: "梵语分词更贵"主要源于部署分词器与语域，而非算法本身；匹配分词后差异主要归结为字符级长度。稳健结论是部署实践中的每义素成本倍数，代码与结果快照已公开。

Abstract: Sanskrit fuses case, number, person and tense into word endings and chains clauses into compounds, so it is information-dense per word. Whether that density survives subword tokenization is a separate question, to be asked per unit of meaning rather than per word. On identical FLORES-200 devtest content, Sanskrit costs 1.774-2.187 times the English tokens under deployed tokenizers with vocabularies of 200,019 ids or more, but only 1.325-1.353 times the Hindi tokens. Against a deployed English tokenizer, Sanskrit-trained BPE arms then look cheaper per proposition than English on contemporary prose (0.887). Against a matched English control, the same algorithm and vocabulary trained on the English side of the same corpus, that flip disappears: at 32,000 and 64,000 pieces all 8 matched pairs, each size-matched arm against both a pair-matched and a byte-matched control, sit above 1.0 on prose with 95% intervals excluding it. The gap closes as the vocabulary grows: at 128,000 pieces the BPE pair reads 0.983 in domain while staying above parity out of domain (1.025) and on FLORES (1.116). The ratio factorises into a character-length ratio and a tokens-per-character ratio, the second near 1 throughout: what survives matched tokenization is character-level length, which Sanskrit prose lacks over English in SLP1 (1.028) and Sanskrit verse has (0.596). The robust statement is about deployed practice: on contemporary prose and on FLORES, with the Sanskrit side in SLP1 against the deployed o200k English pivot, Sanskrit costs 1.831-2.899 English tokens per proposition under the tokenizers people actually ship. Code, the results snapshot and every table here are public.

</details>


### [112] [Judging by the Cover: Cleaning LLM Truthfulness Benchmarks to Avoid Surface-Level Feature Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13003)
*Foad Namjoo,Remy Ogasawara,Amirali Abdullah,Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 二元选择题基准中，正确与错误答案存在表层特征差异，模型可借此"作弊"；作者检测并利用该泄漏，提出清理机制Audit-Prune。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元选择基准假设模型通过推理区分正确答案，但若正确/错误答案在表层特征上系统性不同，模型可绕过推理、仅凭特征就超过随机水平，导致评估失真。

Method: 在TruthfulQA上用六特征逻辑回归分类器检测正确/错误答案的可分离性；验证其他基准也存在类似表层伪影；提出Audit-Prune机制，通过移除最强化泄漏的配对来清理数据。

Result: 简单六特征分类器即可在TruthfulQA上大幅区分正确答案与错误答案；其他基准也存在类似表层伪影；清理后泄漏降至接近随机水平，并发布降低泄漏的TruthfulQA版本。

Conclusion: 表层特征泄漏是可检测且可被下游分类器利用的评估缺陷，Audit-Prune提供了一种可在发布前清洗数据集的通用机制。

Abstract: Binary-choice truth benchmarks ask models to choose between a correct and an incorrect answer, but if the two answers differ systematically in surface-level features, models can exceed chance without performing the intended reasoning. We show that this failure mode is detectable and can be exploited by downstream classifiers. In TruthfulQA, a simple six-feature logistic classifier achieves substantial accuracy in separating correct from incorrect answers. We further show that similar surface-level artifacts are present in additional benchmarks. To counteract this, we developed a general mechanism to clean them by removing the most leakage-reinforcing pairs. We release a version of TruthfulQA with surface-feature leakage reduced close to chance and provide a mechanism, Audit-Prune, so that the datasets can be cleaned before release.

</details>


### [113] [Tasks over Application Manuals: Revealing Gaps in Long-Horizon Procedural Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13005)
*Utkarsh Soni,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Sachin Chandrasekhar,Eugene Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TAM基准，评估LLM在长程程序性推理上的能力，结果显示现有方法表现极差（ICD-10-CM编码1%，联邦量刑15.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多为短程，仅需少量检索或推理步骤，无法反映LLM在遵循数百页复杂、相互依赖指南的真实任务上的可靠性。

Method: 构建TAM基准，从ICD-10-CM临床编码和美国联邦量刑两个领域精选真实任务，配有人工验证标签。每个任务需遵循含数万条规则的权威手册，跨不同章节执行一系列相互依赖步骤得出精确答案。

Result: 评估通用提示方法（RAG、ReAct式提示、agent-harness基线）在GPT-5上的表现，最佳精确匹配率极低：ICD-10-CM编码1%，量刑任务15.5%。

Conclusion: 当前基准可能高估LLM推理能力，并忽视了关键挑战：可靠遵循长规则化流程。TAM数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of natural language tasks, and recent benchmarks suggest that they are increasingly adept at multi-hop reasoning. However, these benchmarks are typically short-horizon, requiring only a small number of retrieval or inference steps, and provide limited evidence of reliability on real-world tasks that involve following manuals spanning hundreds of pages with complex, interdependent guidelines. In this paper, we introduce Tasks over Application Manuals (TAM), a benchmark for evaluating long-horizon procedural reasoning. We construct TAM by curating real-world tasks from two domains: ICD-10-CM clinical coding (mapping medical conditions to diagnostic codes) and U.S. federal sentencing (computing crime sentencing guideline outcomes, specifically offense levels), with human-validated labels. Each task requires following an authoritative manual with tens of thousands of rules and executing a sequence of interdependent steps across different sections to produce an exact answer. We evaluate general-purpose prompting approaches, including retrieval-augmented generation, ReAct-style prompting, and an agent-harness baseline on GPT-5, and find that the best exact-match performance remains extremely low: 1% on ICD-10-CM coding and 15.5% on sentencing tasks. These results show that current benchmarks may overestimate LLM reasoning ability and miss a key challenge: reliably following long, rule-based procedures. The complete TAM data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [114] [Kraken: LLM-based Speech-to-Speech Translation via Low-bitrate VQ and Dual-path Source Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13045)
*Hayato Futami,Hassan Shahmohammadi,Tushar Dhyani,Alkis Koudounas,Raphaël Lafargue,Yosuke Kashiwagi,Quentin Jodelet,Emiru Tsunoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Kraken是一个S2ST模型，用低比特率tokens与Autowave-X解码器，在Qwen3-8B上训练150k小时数据，翻译质量与非语言信息迁移均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音LLM在S2ST中潜力大，但存在两个问题：预测高比特率语音token困难，以及依赖说话人身份与韵律对齐的S2ST训练数据。

Method: 采用基于单层向量量化的低比特率token（重建SSL特征），并引入以源语音为条件的独立token-to-waveform解码器Autowave-X以增强非语言迁移、放宽数据约束。模型Kraken在预训练LLM上增加语音特征输入与低比特率token输出，再接Autowave-X声码器；基于Qwen3-8B，用150k小时多语言多任务语音数据训练。

Result: 翻译质量优于SeamlessM4T-Large v2和Qwen2.5-Omni，且说话人与韵律迁移能力更好。

Conclusion: 低比特率token与源语音条件化解码器可有效提升S2ST性能并缓解数据约束问题。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) has advanced significantly with speech LLMs, offering the potential for joint optimization and preserving non-linguistic information. However, these models struggle with predicting high-bitrate speech tokens in LLMs, and face the challenge of relying on S2ST training data with ideally aligned speaker identity and prosody. We propose using low-bitrate tokens based on single-layer vector quantization, trained to reconstruct self-supervised learning (SSL) features. We also employ a separate token-to-waveform decoder named Autowave-X, which is also conditioned on the source speech to improve non-linguistic transfer, thereby relaxing the training data constraints. With the integration of these techniques, we propose an S2ST model named Kraken, which augments a pre-trained LLM with speech feature inputs and the low-bitrate token outputs, followed by Autowave-X vocoder. We built the model upon Qwen3-8B and trained it using 150k hours of multilingual and multitask speech data. We demonstrated that our model exhibited better translation quality than SeamlessM4T-Large v2 and Qwen2.5-Omni, along with improved speaker and prosody transfer capabilities.

</details>


### [115] [Expert-Space Exploration in MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13058)
*Hongyi He,Zhenghao Lin,Xiao Liu,Peng Cheng,Yan Lu,Yeyun Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESRL，一种面向MoE大模型RL后训练的路由空间探索框架，通过在合理候选池内扰动专家路由并回放路径，提升rollout多样性而不损失质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型的RL研究多关注优化稳定性与训练效率，将专家选择视为固定组件，忽略了路由决定了稀疏计算路径并影响输出分布，可作为rollout多样性的额外来源。

Method: ESRL保留高置信度专家作为锚点，仅在合理候选池内进行随机路由以保留可靠计算路径；根据router熵自适应调整扰动强度以避免过度扰动；记录rollout中的专家路径并在策略优化时回放，缓解路由不匹配。

Result: 在top-K、top-1及共享专家路由的多种MoE骨干和数学、科学、代码任务上均取得最佳表现，且无额外采样或计算开销。Qwen3-30B-A3B上平均Pass@1和Pass@8分别较GRPO提升3.2和4.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 利用MoE特有的路由结构可有效提升RL训练成效，专家利用率与训练动态分析为理解该机制提供了洞见。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training of large language models. Recent advances in RL for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have primarily focused on improving optimization stability and training efficiency, while treating the expert selection as a fixed component. Since routing determines the sparse computation paths that induce output distributions, expert selection offers an additional source of rollout diversity. Through empirical analysis, we find that perturbing expert routing effectively alters model output and increases rollout diversity, which is similar to increasing the decoding temperature. However, direct perturbation can activate unsuitable experts and substantially degrade rollout quality. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning (ESRL), an architecture-aware framework that explicitly explores the expert-routing space of MoE models. ESRL preserves high-confidence experts as anchors, and restricts stochastic routing to a plausible candidate pool, thereby retaining reliable computation paths. The perturbation strength is further adapted according to router entropy to avoid over-perturbation. To mitigate the routing mismatch introduced by perturbation, ESRL records the expert paths used during rollout and replays them during policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that ESRL achieves the best performance across MoE backbones with top-K, top-1, and shared-expert routing, as well as across mathematics, science, and code tasks without additional sampling or computational cost. Specifically, ESRL on Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves the best among all compared methods, improving average Pass@1 and Pass@8 over GRPO by 3.2 and 4.5 percentage points, respectively. Further analyses of expert utilization and training dynamics provide insights into how exploiting MoE-specific routing structure benefits RL training.

</details>


### [116] [Continue, Adapt, or Yield: In-Turn Adaptation to Overlapping Speech in Full-Duplex Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13117)
*Yunqi Lu,Tyler Baumgartner,Nikhil Johri,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Ruben Aguilar,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Duplex Cue 评测框架，衡量全双工语音智能体在"轮内适应"（保持发言同时吸收听者贡献）上的能力，发现 PersonaPlex 模型适应率明显低于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工评测多用"继续说/停止"的二元判断，无法表达人类常见的第三种反应：一边继续说话一边融入听者提供的补充、纠正或澄清。这种轮内适应对自然语音交互至关重要，却缺少评测手段。

Method: 将听者意图（backchannel、协作、打断）与说话者行为（不变继续、轮内适应、让出话轮）分离。基于 300 条未经脚本的英语对话中经人工确认的线索，做单模型案例研究：对比人类录音响应与在重放听者音频时生成的 PersonaPlex 续说，保留 208 对（线索发生时说话者仍在说话且两条件均可评分）。

Result: 在 66 对协作性线索中，人类说话者 68.2% 会进行适应，而 PersonaPlex 仅 34.8%；模型其余情况为不变继续（42.4%）或让出话轮（22.7%）。模型明显偏向"继续不变"或直接停说，缺乏轮内适应。

Conclusion: 评测自然语音交互不仅需衡量智能体是否继续说话，还需衡量其如何回应听者的贡献；Duplex Cue 为此提供了可操作的评测范式，并揭示当前模型在轮内适应上的显著不足。

Abstract: Full-duplex evaluation often emphasizes whether an agent keeps speaking or stops. That binary cannot express a third response humans use routinely: continuing to speak while incorporating what the listener just contributed. The contribution may be a missing word, a correction or a clarification. We introduce Duplex Cue, an evaluation of this \emph{in-turn adaptation} in full-duplex voice agents. Duplex Cue separates listener intent (backchannel, collaboration, or interruption) from speaker behavior: continuing unchanged, adapting within the turn, or yielding. Adaptation includes acknowledgment as well as content revision. In a single-model case study using 300 human-confirmed cues from unscripted English conversations, we compare recorded human responses with PersonaPlex continuations generated while replaying the listener's audio. We retain 208 pairs with the ongoing speaker active at cue onset and a scorable response in each condition. On the 66 collaborative pairs, recorded speakers adapt in 68.2\% of cases, compared with 34.8\% for PersonaPlex. The model otherwise continues unchanged (42.4\%) or yields (22.7\%). These findings show why evaluating natural voice interaction requires measuring how an agent responds to a listener's contribution as well as whether it keeps speaking.

</details>


### [117] [SAS: Simple Attention Sparsification via End-to-End Optimization of Context Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13141)
*Zhiwei Li,Lei Zhu,Hao Gu,Xiang Hu,Yan Wang,Haitao Mi,Sirui Han,Leo Liang,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS)，一种门控稀疏注意力机制，用语言建模损失端到端优化上下文排名，在多种任务上超越现有可训练稀疏注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可训练稀疏注意力方法用轻量selector打分后做hard Top-K选择，阻断梯度，只能蒸馏层wise稠密注意力分布。该排名与固定注意力预算下对预测的影响不对齐，可能浪费有限预算在无用单元上。

Method: SAS将selector的连续分数以log形式注入注意力softmax内的门控；使用归一化softmax门控校准历史上下文与始终保留的当前块；保留连续selector分数以学习相对优先级。并实现内存高效的Triton kernel集成到FlashAttention风格计算中以支持长序列训练。

Result: 在推理、长上下文理解和agentic任务上，SAS在不同注意力预算下持续优于可训练稀疏注意力基线，在紧张预算下增益尤其显著。

Conclusion: SAS通过端到端门控设计实现了更有效的上下文排名，验证了简单设计配合关键选择即可显著提升稀疏注意力在下游任务的表现，并具备长序列训练可行性。

Abstract: Post-training attention sparsification reduces the quadratic cumulative attention cost of pretrained Transformers by selecting a small set of context units (tokens or blocks) for each query. Existing trainable methods usually use a lightweight selector to score context units, followed by hard Top-K selection that blocks gradients from the language modeling loss. Consequently, these methods commonly distill layer-wise dense attention distributions. Although this encourages the selector to rank context units by dense attention weights in the original model, the ranking is not directly aligned with their impact on predictions under a fixed attention budget (i.e., the number of attended context units per query), potentially wasting the limited budget on less useful units. To address this misalignment, we propose Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS), a gated sparse attention mechanism that optimizes context ranking end-to-end with the language modeling loss. The key idea is to inject the selector's continuous scores into attention logits during training, allowing the loss to update the selector through standard backpropagation. We identify several choices crucial for this simple design to work well in practice: placing the gate inside the attention softmax in log form, using normalized softmax gates to calibrate historical context against the always-retained current block, and preserving continuous selector scores so the model learns relative priorities rather than only hard selections. To support long-sequence training, we implement a memory-efficient Triton kernel that integrates SAS into FlashAttention-style computation. Across reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic tasks, SAS consistently outperforms trainable sparse attention baselines across attention budgets, with especially large gains under tight budgets, demonstrating more effective context ranking for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [118] [Type Diversity Enables Transformers to Generalise Compositionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13144)
*Anssi Moisio,Mathias Creutz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Transformer 结构泛化比词汇泛化更难并非固有特性，而是源于数据集类型多样性差异，并通过构建 COGS/SLOG 变体验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现 Transformer 在组合泛化中结构泛化比词汇泛化更难，作者认为这并非 Transformer 固有缺陷，而是由数据集类型多样性差异导致。

Method: 区分词汇与结构泛化，定义类型多样性（构造器数量而非词组合）。使用 Grammatical Framework 创建 COGS 和 SLOG 的语言学多样变体，改变词汇与结构类型多样性。

Result: 类型多样性与组合泛化在词汇和结构测试中同等相关，支持假设；与先前"复合散度解释难度"的命题相矛盾。还探究了其他类型多样性及逻辑语义表面属性等数据集特征的影响。

Conclusion: 结构泛化更难并非 Transformer 固有，而由数据集类型多样性决定；复合散度并非组合泛化难度的解释因素。

Abstract: Compositional generalisation has been divided into lexical and structural generalisation. Previous work has found that structural generalisation is harder than lexical for Transformers. We propose that this difference is not inherent to Transformers, but due to the high diversity of lexical types and low diversity of structural types in the specific datasets of these previous works. By type diversity we mean the number of different constructors of that type, instead of, for example, the specific word combinations that might populate the structure. To test this, we vary the amounts of type diversity of lexical and structural types in previously published datasets. We create linguistically diverse variants of the COGS and SLOG datasets using Grammatical Framework. We find that type diversity correlates with compositional generalisation equally in lexical and structural test cases, supporting our hypothesis. We note a contradiction with the proposition in previous work that compound divergence explains the difficulty in compositional generalisation tasks. We further investigate the effects of other dataset properties on compositional generalisation, such as the diversity of types other than the novel test structure, and surface properties of the logical semantics format.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Deriving the Pure Price of Anarchy for Networked Resource Allocation Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12077)
*Vartika Singh,Philip N. Brown*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文用博弈论方法研究任意信息网络下的多智能体协调，通过线性规划求解基于pPoA的最优局部效用设计，首次解决任意网络的最优效用设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协调中，系统设计者需为智能体分配局部效用函数以引导其行为朝向系统目标，而此前的最优效用设计方法仅考虑完全信息情形，未处理任意信息网络。

Method: 以纯无政府状态代价（pPoA）度量分配效用的性能；构建线性规划，针对任意信息网络和任意系统目标求解最优pPoA，从而推导最优效用函数，推广了仅限完全信息的既有方法。

Result: 对超模目标函数，证明完全拒绝通信的效用设计无论原信息网络如何都是最优的（反直觉）；对次模系统目标，数值分析表明最优效用设计对通信故障具有鲁棒性；当系统目标为加权最大覆盖时，边际贡献效用设计可证明在多种信息网络上优化pPoA。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现任意网络下的最优效用设计，所提线性规划框架具有通用性，并为超模、次模及加权最大覆盖等目标提供了理论或数值上的最优性/鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: This work considers multi-agent coordination with arbitrary information networks among the agents using a game-theoretic approach. A system designer aims to assign local utility functions to the agents to guide their actions toward a desired system objective. The performance of the assigned local utilities is measured by the well known pure price of anarchy (pPoA) metric that equals the ratio of the system objective at the worst pure Nash equilibrium of the corresponding game to the optimal system objective. Our aim is to derive the utility functions which optimize the pPoA-based performance guarantees for any given information network and system objective. We develop a linear program that derives the optimal pPoA for any arbitrary information network and arbitrary system objective. Our work is the first to solve optimal utility design for arbitrary networks; our techniques generalize previous approaches which considered only the full-information setting. For supermodular objective functions, we prove that counterintuitively, a fully communication-denied utility design is optimal irrespective of the original information network. For submodular system objectives, an exhaustive numerical analysis suggests that the optimal utility design is robust to communication failures even for this case. When the system objective is weighted maximum coverage, the marginal contribution utility design provably optimizes the pPoA for a wide variety of information networks of interest.

</details>


### [120] [Opening the Strategic Pandora Box: Conditional Transaction Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12197)
*Yonatan Sompolinsky,Ori Newman,Xintong Wang,David Parkes*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文形式化条件交易引擎中每次调用的机制设计问题，提出 Strategic Pandora 模型，并设计 RW 与 RWSP 机制，在动态 No-Betting Revenue 基准下实现常数比例收益保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件交易引擎需为离线用户执行条件指令，但每次评估条件会延迟最终写入，因此需要决定待评估条件的顺序；同时用户私下知晓成功概率与写入价值，存在策略性报告问题。

Method: 将问题建模为 Strategic Pandora——独立 Bernoulli 盒子的折现 Pandora's box 变体；提出 reported-Weitzman (RW) 与 reported-values second-price (RWSP) 机制；为无共同先验下的收益比较引入 dynamic No-Betting Revenue (NBR) 基准。

Result: 在所述竞争与均衡条件下，RW 或 RWSP 的每个合格纯策略均衡都能获得其对应动态 NBR 基准的常数比例收益。

Conclusion: 该工作为条件交易机制提供了形式化框架和具有收益保证的机制设计，拓展了 Pandora's box 与无先验收益基准的分析。

Abstract: Conditional transaction engines (CTEs) execute conditional instructions for offline users. This paper formalizes the mechanism-design problem within each engine invocation. A conditional transaction mechanism (CTM) decides which pending conditions to evaluate first because each evaluation delays the eventual write. We model this problem as Strategic Pandora, a discounted variant of the Pandora's box model with independent Bernoulli boxes. Agents report privately assessed success probabilities and, in the full model, values for the write action. We propose the reported-Weitzman mechanism (RW) and the reported-values second-price mechanism (RWSP). To compare revenue without a common prior, we introduce dynamic No-Betting Revenue. Under the stated competition and equilibrium conditions, every qualifying pure equilibrium of RW or RWSP earns a constant fraction of its corresponding dynamic NBR benchmark.

</details>


### [121] [On the Fragility of Worst-Case Nash Equilibria in Atomic Congestion Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12220)
*Colton Hill,Brandon Collins,Philip N. Brown*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 在激励化的原子拥堵博弈中，智能出行系统的均衡效率与智能体对自身决策的满意度（相对最优配置）密切相关：满意度高则均衡效率高，二者不能同时为差或同时为优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能出行系统的整体性能取决于自私的、共享资源的智能体的涌现行为，现有研究虽表明通过激励可影响行为以最小化系统成本，但最坏情形均衡相比系统最优可能非常低效，而智能体对这些最坏情形决策是否"满意"尚不清楚。

Method: 针对激励化原子拥堵博弈，分析均衡处智能体的聚合满意度（将其均衡行动与最优配置中的行动对比）与对应系统成本效率之间的关系，并考察最坏情形纳什均衡的性质。

Result: 在任意激励化原子拥堵博弈中，若智能体对其均衡选择非常满意，则该均衡必然相对高效；且最坏情形纳什均衡是脆弱的，每个智能体在其最坏情形均衡行动与系统最优配置行动之间无差异。

Conclusion: 在均衡处，要么智能体对决策高度满意，要么决策高度低效，但二者不能同时成立。这补充了其他博弈类别的近期结果，表明最坏情形均衡效率保证仅在智能体对决策无差异时才会出现。

Abstract: Aggregate performance in smart mobility systems depends heavily on the emergent behavior of selfish, resource-sharing agents that participate within the systems. As a result, recent work has focused on how a system designer can leverage incentives to influence behavior so that system cost (e.g., traffic congestion) is minimized. These results show that worst-case equilibria can be quite inefficient compared to system-optimal allocations. However, it is unclear to what extent agents are ``satisfied'' with their decisions in these worst-case scenarios. We demonstrate that in any incentivized atomic congestion game, agents' aggregate satisfaction at equilibrium (relative to their actions in an optimal allocation) is correlated with the efficiency of the corresponding system cost, in the sense that if agents are very satisfied with their equilibrium choices, the equilibrium must be relatively efficient. Further, we show that worst-case Nash equilibria are fragile, as every agent is indifferent between their action in a worst-case equilibrium and their action in a system-optimal allocation. In summary, at equilibrium, either agents are highly satisfied with their decisions or their decisions are highly inefficient, but both cannot be true simultaneously. This work adds to recent results for other classes of games which indicate that worst-case equilibrium efficiency guarantees only occur when agents are indifferent about their decisions.

</details>


### [122] [The Fixed Server Locality Gap of Count Load Assignment Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12572)
*Hao Li,Mengfan Ma*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究异构工作负载、非负分配成本与私有资格菜单下共享服务器的不可分客户定价博弈，证明边际贡献定价下每车辆/服务器容量M对应的无政府状态代价随工作负载比上限κ增长为Θ_M(κ^{1-2^{-M}})，并给出UAV-地面服务器链式实现及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边际贡献定价使社会目标成为精确势函数，但不能保证所有稳定分配都是高效的。作者旨在刻画这种低效程度，即无政府状态代价（PoA）如何随负载异质性和服务器容量上限变化。

Method: 研究不可分客户、异构工作负载、任意非负分配成本与私有资格菜单；每服务器社会成本为占用数乘以聚合工作负载及服务器特定系数。对固定服务器容量M，结合共同乘子证书、源加权残差不等式与有序矩递推证明PoA上界；构造匹配下界的链式族，并用移动UAV客户与固定地面服务器实现，证明精确航点消除和可计算的分量细化。

Result: 证明固定M下PoA为Θ_M(κ^{1-2^{-M}})，上界对任意均衡和任意可行比较成立，无需无环比较或强连通限制。UAV链式族实现紧性。枚举2,800个合成博弈发现低效均衡，270组参数化实例评估相对可行多起点参考的增量延迟。紧性针对固定M的异质性指数，而非匹配常系数或最坏情况频率。

Conclusion: 边际贡献定价不足以保证稳定分配高效，其低效上界由κ^{1-2^{-M}}精确刻画；该结果建立了与调度/拥塞边界的联系，指明哪些既有界可迁移，并通过UAV实现与合成实验验证理论。

Abstract: Marginal-contribution pricing makes the social objective an exact potential, but does not ensure that every stable assignment is efficient. We study indivisible clients with heterogeneous workloads, arbitrary nonnegative assignment costs, and private eligibility menus over a fixed number of shared servers and optional local execution. Each server's social cost is its occupancy multiplied by its aggregate workload and a server-specific coefficient. For every fixed server cap $M$, we prove that the price of anarchy over this class is $Θ_M(κ^{1-2^{-M}})$ as the workload-ratio cap $κ$ grows. The upper bound applies to every equilibrium and every feasible comparison, without an acyclic-comparison or strongly-connected-component restriction. Its proof combines a common-multiplier certificate, a source-weighted residual inequality and an ordered moment recurrence. A matching chain family has a realization by mobile unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) clients and fixed ground servers, with positive altitude, positive-width coverage and an explicitly paid wireless baseline. We prove exact waypoint elimination and give an input-computable component refinement. Scheduling and congestion identities delimit which established bounds transfer. Exact enumeration of 2,800 synthetic games identifies inefficient equilibria, while 270 primary-parameterized synthetic instance-budget records evaluate incremental latency relative to a feasible multistart reference. Tightness concerns the heterogeneity exponent for fixed $M$, not matching leading constants or measured frequency of the worst-case configurations.

</details>


### [123] [Universally truthful mechanisms for scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12621)
*Georgios Anastasiadis,George Christodoulou,Elias Koutsoupias,Annamaria Kovacs,Conrad Schecker*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究在n台无关机器上调度m个作业的普适真实随机机制的近似比下界，证明离散支撑下界为n/12-o(n)，并与近似比n/2+o(n)的机制在常数因子内匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨无关机器调度中普适真实随机机制能达到的最优近似比，明确其理论极限。

Method: 针对概率分布具有离散支撑的普适真实随机机制，证明其期望近似比的下界；并构造一个近似比为n/2+o(n)的机制。

Result: 任何此类机制无法达到小于n/12-o(n)的近似比；构造的机制达到n/2+o(n)，在常数因子内匹配下界。

Conclusion: 离散支撑的普适真实随机机制的近似比紧确到常数因子，位于n/12-o(n)与n/2+o(n)之间。

Abstract: We consider universally truthful randomized mechanisms for the problem of scheduling $m$ jobs on $n$ unrelated machines. We prove a lower bound on the expected approximation ratio of every such mechanism whose probability distribution has discrete support. We show that no universally truthful randomized mechanism in this class can achieve approximation ratio smaller than $n/12 - o(n)$ with respect to the optimal makespan. We match this, up to a constant factor, by a mechanism with approximation ratio $n/2 + o(n)$.

</details>


### [124] [A Randomized $\frac32$-Approximation for Strategic Facility Location on a Circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12792)
*Hau Chan,Jianan Lin,Chenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究圆上单设施选址的策略防诈机制，提出奇偶依赖混合机制，达到3/2近似比，并改进下界至11/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在圆上以功利社会成本为目标进行单设施选址时，需要策略防诈机制；已有工作仅覆盖奇数n且近似比上界为7/4，偶数n缺乏保证，下界也较弱。

Method: 分析一种奇偶依赖机制：奇数n时等概率混合Random Dictator（RD）与Proportional Circle Distance（PCD）；偶数n时混合RD与随机删除扩展的PCD（先均匀随机删除一个agent再对剩余agent应用PCD）。

Result: 该机制是策略防诈的，且对所有n≥3达到3/2近似比；改进了Rogowski与Dziubiński仅适用于奇数n的7/4上界，并扩展到偶数n；同时给出任意随机策略防诈机制近似比下界11/10，优于Meir之前的1.0456。

Conclusion: 为圆上单设施功利选址提供了统一且更优的策略防诈随机机制与更紧的下界，缩小了上下界差距。

Abstract: We study strategyproof mechanisms for locating a single facility on a circle so as to serve a set of strategic agents under the utilitarian social cost objective. We analyze a simple parity-dependent mechanism that randomizes between the Random Dictator (RD) mechanism and the Proportional Circle Distance (PCD) mechanism. For an odd number \(n\) of agents, this mechanism mixes RD and PCD with equal probability, as originally proposed by Rogowski and Dziubi{ń}ski (IJCAI 2025). For even \(n\), it mixes RD with a random-deletion extension of PCD, in which one agent is removed uniformly at random before PCD is applied to the remaining agents. Our main result shows that this mechanism is strategyproof and achieves an approximation ratio of \(\frac32\) for every \(n\ge 3\). This improves the \(\frac74\) upper bound of Rogowski and Dziubi{ń}ski, which applied only to odd \(n\), and extends the guarantee to even numbers of agents. Finally, we establish a lower bound of \(\frac{11}{10}\) on the approximation ratio of any randomized strategyproof mechanism, improving on the previous lower bound of \(1.0456\) due to Meir (SAGT 2019).

</details>


### [125] [Learning-Augmented Strategyproof Facility Location in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $\ell_p$ Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12829)
*Hau Chan,Jianan Lin,Chenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究带预测的设施选址机制设计，分析坐标中位数机制（CMP）在一致性与鲁棒性上的近似保证，给出二维精确界与高维渐近紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设施选址中引入预测（最优位置）以提升性能，但需在策略防欺诈前提下量化预测正确与任意预测下的表现（一致性与鲁棒性）。

Method: 分析CMP机制：在预测最优位置加入cn个虚拟agent后取坐标逐维中位数。二维下用各坐标中位数条件比较机制代价与最优代价，并用仅三个不同位置的实例证明紧性；高维下构造实例族证明上界的渐近紧性。

Result: d=2时对每个p∈[1,∞]给出精确保证；1&lt;p&lt;∞时一致性与鲁棒性分别为 (1+((1-c)/(1+c))^(p/(p-1)))^((p-1)/p) 和 (1+((1+c)/(1-c))^(p/(p-1)))^((p-1)/p)。对1&lt;p&lt;∞给出任意维度d≥1的维数无关上界，d→∞时渐近紧；并给出p=1的精确界与p=∞的渐近紧界。

Conclusion: 高维结果在c=0时恢复Gravin和Jia（STOC 2025）的无预测界，并在p=2时恢复其任意维欧氏空间中的学习增强界。

Abstract: We study learning-augmented mechanism design for locating a single facility in $\mathbb{R}^d$ to minimize the sum of the agents' $\ell_p$ distances to the facility. We analyze the coordinate-wise median with predictions (CMP) mechanism, which adds $cn$ virtual agents at a predicted optimal facility location to the $n$ reported locations and returns their coordinate-wise median. The parameter $c\in[0,1)$ represents confidence in the prediction; CMP is known to be strategyproof when both are fixed independently of the reports. We determine its approximation guarantees under correct predictions (consistency) and arbitrary predictions (robustness) in two settings. First, for $d=2$, we establish exact guarantees for every $p\in[1,+\infty]$. For $1<p<+\infty$, the consistency and robustness are respectively $\Bigl(1+\bigl(\frac{1-c}{1+c}\bigr)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}\Bigr)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}$ and $\Bigl(1+\bigl(\frac{1+c}{1-c}\bigr)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}\Bigr)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}$. We use the median condition in each coordinate to compare the mechanism's cost with the optimal cost, and establish tightness using instances with agents at only three distinct locations.
  Second, for $1<p<+\infty$, we obtain dimension-independent consistency and robustness upper bounds valid for every $d\ge1$. For each fixed $p$ and $c$, we construct families of instances whose ratios approach the respective upper bounds as $d\to\infty$, proving asymptotic tightness. We also establish exact guarantees for $p=1$ in every dimension and asymptotically tight bounds for $p=+\infty$. Our high-dimensional results recover the prediction-free bounds of Gravin and Jia (STOC 2025) when $c=0$ and their learning-augmented bounds in arbitrary-dimensional Euclidean spaces when $p=2$.

</details>


### [126] [The possibility of solving a 3x3 Rubik's Cube under 2 seconds - Optimizing block building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12946)
*Chung To Kong*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出"AK-cubie方法"，一种在短时间内构建近最优积木(block-building)步骤序列的系统方法，并附带训练软件，服务魔方速拧选手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔方速拧中block building是核心技能，但缺乏系统化的理解与分析手段；回看录像只能看到结果而非内在机制。

Method: 提出AK-cubie方法（简称cubie method），在较短计算时间内生成近最优的积木构建步骤序列，并配套供选手训练pre-lookahead与积木技能的软件。

Result: 可在短计算时间内得到近最优的积木构建步骤序列，并支持选手训练。

Conclusion: 为积木构建技术提供了系统化方法，期望助力未来打破2秒世界纪录。

Abstract: Block building is an essential skill in speedcubing competitions. However, there is currently no systematic method to understand or analyze block-building techniques. Rewatching gameplay reveals only the outcome, not the underlying mechanism. This paper presents a systematic approach, the "AK-cubie method" (or "cubie method" for short), for constructing near-optimal block-building move sequences within a short computation time. Speedcubers can use the accompanying software to train their look-ahead and block-building skills. We hope this work will contribute to breaking the 2-second world record in the near future.

</details>


### [127] [Mitigating Emergent Collusion in LLM Pricing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13037)
*Abdullah Garra*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 该论文复现了LLM定价代理在重复寡头市场中产生超竞争结果的现象（用DeepSeek-V3.1），并评估三种监管干预的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LLM定价代理在未被明确指示合谋的情况下，会在重复寡头环境中产生超竞争结果，需要探索有效的监管干预手段。

Method: 用DeepSeek-V3.1复现Fish等人的定性提示敏感性效应（P1 vs P2），并测试三种干预：纯提示警告、Harrington式预期损害赔偿支付监管、主动随机进入者。

Result: P1提示产生显著更高价格和利润（但垄断性弱于原GPT-4结果）；提示警告减少但未消除高于Nash的定价；Harrington监管使P1接近双寡头Nash基准并消除P1-P2显著差异；主动进入者效果最强，使两个提示均低于随机进入的Nash基准。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，改变激励或市场参与的干预比单纯的提示警告更能有效降低超竞争定价。

Abstract: Recent work shows that LLM-based pricing agents can produce supracompetitive outcomes in repeated oligopoly environments without being explicitly instructed to collude. We reproduce the qualitative prompt-sensitivity effect of Fish et al. using DeepSeek-V3.1: the P1 prompt produces significantly higher prices and profits than P2, although our outcomes are less monopoly-like than the original GPT-4 results. We then evaluate three regulatory interventions: a prompt-only warning, a Harrington-inspired expected-damages payoff regulator, and an active random entrant. The prompt-only regulator reduces but does not eliminate above-Nash pricing. The Harrington regulator brings P1 outcomes close to the duopoly Nash benchmark and removes the statistically significant P1--P2 gap. The active entrant produces the strongest effect, pushing both prompts below the appropriate random-entrant Nash benchmark. Overall, our experiments provide preliminary evidence that interventions that alter incentives or market participation can reduce supracompetitive pricing more effectively than prompt warnings alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Position: Recommender Systems Should Move Beyond Platform-Centric Ranking toward Personal Agent-Mediated Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11942)
*Haohan Yuan,Peng He,Dan Zhang,Jianpeng Liang,Junning Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"个人智能体中介推荐"(PAMR)新范式,主张推荐系统的核心瓶颈已从偏好建模转向对证据获取与披露的控制权,即从平台侧物品排序转向用户侧证据中介。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统被框定为排序系统:平台观察用户、构建候选集并替用户选择物品。这种框架掩盖了更深层的控制分配问题——平台同时决定了候选准入、证据边界、解释方式以及从用户需求到推荐输出的路径。因此,推荐的下一瓶颈不仅是偏好建模,更是对证据获取与披露的控制。

Method: 作为立场论文,作者:1) 将PAMR定义为一个新的推荐范式,由面向用户的个人智能体代表用户在分布式来源中完成证据的发现、过滤、聚合与治理;2) 建立该范式的边界标准;3) 识别其核心中介决策;4) 提出以中介为中心的评价框架。并通过在困难Yelp餐厅推荐任务上的概念验证研究进行说明。

Result: 在困难Yelp餐厅推荐任务的概念验证研究中,在共享同一LLM排序器的条件下,来源选择与受控披露提供了最强的效用—可追溯性—曝光度—成本(utility–traceability–exposure–cost)操作点。

Conclusion: 推荐的核心转变不是从一个排序模型换到另一个,而是从平台侧物品排序转向用户侧证据中介。PAMR为推荐研究提供了以证据中介为中心的新方向与评价框架。

Abstract: Recommender systems are usually framed as ranking systems: platforms observe users, construct candidate sets, and select items on their behalf. This framing hides a deeper allocation of control, in which platforms also determine candidate access, evidence boundaries, explanations, and the path from user need to recommended output. We argue that the next bottleneck in recommendation is not only preference modeling, but control over evidence acquisition and disclosure. We argue for \textbf{Personal Agent-Mediated Recommendation} (PAMR), a paradigm in which a user-facing personal agent represents the user in discovering, filtering, aggregating, and governing recommendation evidence across distributed sources. The central shift is not simply from one ranking model to another, but from platform-side item ranking to user-side evidence mediation. As a position paper, we define PAMR as a new recommendation paradigm, establish its boundary criteria, identify its core mediation decisions, and propose a mediation-centered evaluation framework. A proof-of-concept study on hard Yelp restaurant recommendation tasks further shows that, under a shared LLM ranker, source selection and controlled disclosure provide the strongest observed utility--traceability--exposure--cost operating point.

</details>


### [129] [PinDCO: Whole-Page Aware Dynamic Creative Optimization at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11943)
*Yu Hao,Yuchun Li,Peimeng Sui,Meilin Liu,Tianyuan Cui,Hao Li,Zicong Zhou,Akanksha Baid*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PinDCO是Pinterest提出并上线的广告创意检索与选择系统，核心为创意组件融合网络（CCFN），并引入像素感知调整模块（PAM）等优化，实现大规模动态创意优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI加速了广告创意生成，导致单次投放的候选创意数量激增，亟需在严苛的延迟和成本约束下，将创意与最相关受众匹配的可扩展动态创意优化（DCO）系统。

Method: 提出创意组件融合网络CCFN：对图片、标题、布局等每个创意组件用独立塔建模并采用组件特定超参；融合各组件表示，在广告级预测条件下预测创意级得分；通过探索-利用策略提升训练数据质量。针对瀑布流网格布局引入像素感知调整模块PAM，按创意尺寸调整得分以提升屏幕空间利用与整页效果；并采用轻量预选模型早期剪枝，结合缓存与动态批处理优化服务效率。

Result: 大量离线分析与在线A/B实验验证有效性，广告点击率（CTR）提升+3.09%，且整页指标为正。

Conclusion: PinDCO表现强劲，已在Pinterest广告平台正式上线。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have substantially accelerated the creation of high-quality ad creatives, dramatically expanding the number of candidate variants per campaign. This shift increases the need for scalable dynamic creative optimization (DCO) systems that can match creatives to the most relevant audiences under stringent latency and cost constraints. We present PinDCO, a production DCO system for ad creative retrieval and selection on Pinterest, a billion-scale visual discovery platform. PinDCO is built around a Creative Component Fusion Network (CCFN) that performs dynamic creative scoring by modeling each creative component (e.g., image, title, layout) with a dedicated tower, using component-specific hyperparameters to account for differing modeling complexity. The component representations are fused to predict a creative-level score conditioned on the ad-level prediction, and we improve training data quality via an exploration-exploitation strategy. To account for Pinterest's waterfall grid layout, where a creative's rendered size affects nearby content and session-level engagement, we introduce a Pixel-aware Adjustment Module(PAM) that adjusts scores based on creative size to encourage efficient screen real-estate utilization and better whole-page outcomes. To support the large volume of creative candidates, we further employ a lightweight pre-selection model for early pruning, and optimize serving efficiency through caching and dynamic batching. Extensive offline analyses and online A/B experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of PinDCO, yielding a +3.09% lift in ad Click-Through Rate(CTR) with positive whole-page metrics. With the strong performance, we launched PinDCO in the Pinterest Ads platform.

</details>


### [130] [Who Are We Recommending To? Recommender Systems in the Agentic Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11945)
*Himan Abdollahpouri,Kyle Kretschman,Sai Ravindranath,Jackie Doremus,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 推荐系统正从"人类直接消费推荐"转向"AI代理作为推荐消费者"的分化范式，本文提出委托光谱与新的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二十年来推荐系统假设由人类直接接收、解读并执行推荐。但基于大语言模型的AI代理正代表用户浏览、比较、谈判和交易，动摇了这一假设，引出核心问题：推荐的真正接收者是谁？

Method: 这是一篇立场论文。作者论证推荐范式正发生分化：在可委托场景（日常采购、旅行、受约束的交易任务）中，代理成为主要操作消费者；在体验型场景（娱乐、艺术等高主观或高风险选择）中，人类仍是相关性的最终裁判。为此提出"委托光谱"，用偏好可指定性、结果可验证性、决策风险等维度刻画推荐场景，并勾勒研究议程。

Result: 提出委托光谱框架及跨代理偏好建模、双受众优化、新兴代理注意力经济的研究议程，并讨论该转变对推荐系统设计与评估的影响。

Conclusion: 推荐系统需针对代理消费者与人类消费者两类受众重新设计优化目标、交互协议与评估标准，本文为这一分化趋势提供了理论框架与研究路线。

Abstract: For two decades, recommender systems have been designed under the assumption that a human directly consumes each recommendation: receiving, interpreting, and acting upon it. The emergence of AI agents powered by large language models challenges this assumption. In the emerging Agentic Web [ 28 ], autonomous agents increasingly act on behalf of users, e.g., browsing, comparing, negotiating, and executing transactions, raising a central question: who is the receiver of a recommendation? In this position paper, we argue that the recommendation paradigm is undergoing a bifurcation. In delegable contexts, such as routine purchases, travel, and constrained transactional tasks, the primary operational consumer of recommendations is shifting from the human to the agent, requiring new optimization objectives, interaction protocols, and evaluation criteria. In experiential contexts, such as entertainment, art, and other subjective or high-stakes choices, humans remain the final judge of relevance, though agents may assist through pre-filtering and curation. We introduce a delegation spectrum that characterizes recommendation contexts along factors such as preference specifiability, outcome verifiability, and decision stakes, and we outline a research agenda spanning agent preference modeling, dual-audience optimization, and the emerging agent attention economy. We further discuss the implications of this shift for the design and evaluation of recommender systems

</details>


### [131] [MemRetriever: Learning to Search, Reflect, and Retrieve from Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11951)
*Ruiyang Jiang,Chunyu Li,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MemRetriever代理式检索模型，将记忆访问建模为多步搜索决策过程，结合监督热启动与GRPO强化学习，在多个基准上优于静态检索和仅监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数记忆系统采用静态top-k检索：单次查询、固定数量返回、直接送入下游模型，导致漏检跨会话证据、引入无关内容、浪费上下文，尤其在多跳、时序和知识更新问题上表现不佳。

Method: 将记忆访问视为多步搜索过程，每步基于当前证据推理并选择并行搜索（广探索）、串行搜索（定向补全）或反思去噪（过滤与证据评估），证据充分即停止；构建ReAct式搜索-记忆轨迹做监督热启动，再用Group Relative Policy Optimization优化，奖励涵盖证据覆盖、降噪、答案充分性与高效终止。

Result: 在LOCOMO、LongMemEval、HotpotQA、MuSiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA上一致优于静态检索与仅监督基线；MemRetriever-4B-RL在相同流程下于LongMemEval主要检索指标上超过DeepSeek-v4-Flash，在MuSiQue上取得对比方法中最佳结果。

Conclusion: 决策逻辑与存储后端解耦，可运行于外部知识库和向量数据库；表明一个负责规划、搜索、证据过滤与停止判断的中间决策层能同时提升长期记忆检索与知识密集型问答。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables personalized agents, but its value depends on retrieving the right evidence at the right time. Most memory systems use static top-k retrieval: they issue one query, return a fixed number of memories, and pass them directly to a downstream model. This approach can miss evidence distributed across sessions, introduce irrelevant content, and waste context, especially for multi-hop, temporal, and knowledge-update questions. We present MemRetriever, an agentic retrieval model that treats memory access as a multi-step search process. At each step, MemRetriever reasons over the current evidence and selects parallel search for broad exploration, serial search for targeted completion, or reflection and denoising for filtering and evidence assessment. It stops when the retained evidence is sufficient for downstream answering. We construct ReAct-style search-memory trajectories for supervised warm-start training and further optimize the model with Group Relative Policy Optimization. The reward design encourages evidence coverage, noise reduction, answer sufficiency, and efficient termination. Experiments on LOCOMO, LongMemEval, HotpotQA, MuSiQue, and 2WikiMultiHopQA show consistent improvements over static retrieval and supervised-only baselines. MemRetriever-4B-RL also outperforms DeepSeek-v4-Flash on the main LongMemEval retrieval metrics under the same pipeline and achieves the strongest results among the compared methods on MuSiQue. Because its decision logic is independent of the storage backend, MemRetriever can also operate over external knowledge bases and vector databases. These results show that an intermediate decision layer that plans, searches, filters evidence, and determines when to stop can improve both long-term memory retrieval and knowledge-intensive question answering.

</details>


### [132] [InitGen: Candidate Generation for Interaction Initiation in Intelligent Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11953)
*Ruize Shi,Jinhua Chen,Hong Huang,Ziniu Chen,Ruike Zhang,Jianxun Shi,Yitao Chen,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出InitGen框架，用于智能助手交互启动阶段的候选查询生成，通过加权偏好优化与滚动窗口更新，已在OPPO小布助手部署，CTR从0.95%提升至1.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互启动需在严格延迟预算内结合动态上下文生成候选查询，但生成候选多于实际展示，反馈部分且难以归属单个查询；同时高活跃用户在训练中占主导，影响学习效果。

Method: InitGen联合生成一组候选查询，并通过加权偏好优化将生成集合与用户反馈对齐；样本权重来自用户活跃度与下游排序分数，活跃度权重抑制高活跃用户主导，排序分数作为反馈可靠性估计；采用滚动窗口更新策略纳入近期交互数据。

Result: 在线A/B测试中，CTR从0.95%提升至1.61%（相对提升69.1%），查询曝光量提升17.9%；完整候选集生成耗时低于180 ms，已全面部署于月活超1.5亿的OPPO小布助手。

Conclusion: InitGen有效解决了交互启动场景下候选生成的部分反馈与延迟约束问题，显著提升线上性能并实现大规模生产部署。

Abstract: Interaction initiation refers to presenting multiple candidate queries when a user opens an intelligent assistant before expressing any intent for the current session. In production, candidate generation incorporates dynamic context and produces all candidates within a strict latency budget. Learning from user feedback is also difficult since the generator usually produces more candidates than are finally displayed. After downstream filtering and ranking, only a subset is exposed to users, so the observed feedback is partial and cannot be reliably assigned to individual queries. We present InitGen, a framework for candidate generation that is deployed in the interaction initiation pipeline of OPPO's Xiaobu Assistant. InitGen generates a set of candidate queries jointly and aligns the generated set with user feedback through weighted preference optimization. The sample weights are derived from user activity and downstream ranking scores. The activity weight reduces the dominance of highly active users during training, while the ranking score is used as a practical estimate of the reliability of the observed feedback. InitGen also uses a rolling window update strategy to incorporate recent interaction data into periodic model updates. In an online A/B test against a strong production baseline, InitGen improves the click-through rate from 0.95% to 1.61%, corresponding to a relative improvement of 69.1%, and increases query exposure by 17.9% under the same traffic allocation. InitGen generates the complete candidate set within 180 ms and has been fully deployed in OPPO's Xiaobu Assistant, which serves over 150 million monthly active users.

</details>


### [133] [Recommendation Retrievers Need Verifiers: Universal Generative Reranking for Sequential Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12270)
*Benyu Zhang,Qiang Zhang,Rui Li,Qunshu Zhang,Devansh Tandon,Neeraj Bhatia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出轻量级生成式验证器，对检索器top-K候选进行事后验证，将深层相关项提升进短名单，无需重训练即可提升Recall@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段推荐中首阶段只转发有限前缀，相关项可能排在检索列表较深处却未进入短名单；需要在不重训练、不替换检索器的前提下提升前缀召回。

Method: 引入轻量生成式验证器：给定检索器状态与候选物品，通过物品标识token的似然打分；用下一token交叉熵事后训练，无需负采样或候选池；推理时仅对top-K打分；接口最小化，支持任意固定分词。

Result: 在Amazon商品推荐与YaMBDa音乐推荐上，同一训练方案提升SASRec、GRU4Rec、NextItNet、MiniOneRec的Recall@10；消融表明提升不能仅归因于向检索器注入物品内容特征。

Conclusion: 验证器作为事后输出侧适配机制有效，可在不重训练检索器情况下提升首阶段短名单覆盖。

Abstract: First-stage recommenders in multi-stage systems produce a ranked candidate list from which a limited prefix is forwarded to downstream rankers. Because each forwarded item must be processed by more expensive ranking stages, this shortlist cannot be arbitrarily large. The first-stage objective is therefore high coverage of relevant items within the forwarded prefix, commonly measured by Recall@$k$. A relevant item may be available deeper in the retrieved list but absent from the shorter prefix that is actually consumed. This paper studies post-hoc verification for promoting such candidates into the consumed shortlist without retraining or replacing the retriever.
  We introduce a lightweight generative verifier for retrieval models. Given a retriever state and a candidate item, the verifier scores the item through the likelihood of its identifier tokens. It is trained post hoc with next-token cross entropy, requires no sampled negatives or candidate pool during training, and scores only the retriever's top-$K$ candidates at inference. The interface is minimal: the retriever supplies a query state and candidate items, and the item representation can use any fixed tokenization.
  Across Amazon product recommendation and YaMBDa music recommendation, the same verifier training recipe improves Recall@10 for SASRec, GRU4Rec, NextItNet, and MiniOneRec. Ablations show that the improvements are not explained solely by injecting item-content features into the retriever, supporting verification as a post-hoc output-side adaptation mechanism.

</details>


### [134] [ChronicleRec: Pre-training Temporally Anchored Tokens for Lifelong User Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12375)
*Chengkai Huang,Yubin Sheng,Liang Guo,Haoxi Liu,Junwei Pan,Shangyu Zhang,Zhixiang Feng,Chao Zhou,Chengguo Yin,Lina Yao,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 ChronicleRec 框架，将超长用户行为序列一次性压缩为按时间排序的 Chronicle Tokens，实现目标无关的缓存复用，解耦超长序列建模与在线候选打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接输入数千条历史行为计算代价过高，截断会丢失长程信号；现有终身兴趣方法检索目标相关行为，将长序列建模与候选打分耦合且在线重复计算；近期目标无关压缩方法在序列末端追加 query token 并用双向编码，导致摘要无序、冗余并忽视时序结构。

Method: 1) 采用 recency-aware 多粒度合并，保留近期行为、粗化远期历史；2) 将 query token 与合并序列交错并用因果编码器，使每个 query 仅汇总其时间锚点之前的历史；3) 多视野设计在不同并行分支掩蔽不同近期窗口以学习互补长程兴趣；4) 用 mask-and-predict 目标预训练压缩器，从压缩的旧历史重建被掩蔽的近期行为，对齐历史信号与近期意图；5) Chronicle Tokens 目标无关，可按用户缓存。

Result: 在 KuaiRand 和 Tencent AdLive 上优于近期窗口与单次压缩基线，接近全注意力性能；Token 分析显示表征具有时序组织性与互补性；七天在线 A/B 测试确认显著的生产收益。

Conclusion: ChronicleRec 通过时间有序、目标无关的压缩 token 与缓存机制，在保证效果接近全注意力的同时显著降低在线成本，并已在真实工业场景验证有效。

Abstract: Modeling ultra-long user behavior sequences is crucial for industrial recommendation and online advertising, yet directly feeding thousands of historical actions into ranking models is computationally prohibitive, while truncation discards long-range signals. Existing lifelong-interest methods retrieve target-relevant behaviors for each candidate, coupling long-sequence modeling with candidate scoring and repeated online cost. Recent target-independent compression methods enable cached user summaries, but often append query tokens at the sequence end and use bidirectional encoding, producing unordered and redundant summaries that overlook temporal structure. We propose ChronicleRec, a pre-train-and-transfer framework that compresses an ultra-long behavior sequence once into a chronologically ordered set of Chronicle Tokens. ChronicleRec applies a recency-aware multi-granularity merge, preserving recent behaviors while coarsening distant history. It then interleaves query tokens with the merged sequence and uses a causal encoder, so each query summarizes only the history before its temporal anchor. A multi-horizon design masks different recent-history windows across parallel branches to learn complementary long-range interests. The compressor is pre-trained with a mask-and-predict objective that reconstructs held-out recent behaviors from compressed older history, aligning historical signals with near-present intent. Since Chronicle Tokens are target-independent, they can be cached per user, decoupling ultra-long sequence modeling from online candidate scoring. Experiments on KuaiRand and Tencent AdLive show that ChronicleRec outperforms recent-window and single-pass compression baselines while approaching full-attention performance. Token analyses reveal temporally organized and complementary representations, and a seven-day online A/B test confirms significant production gains.

</details>


### [135] [Preference-Drift-Aware Subsequence Learning and Hierarchical Context Fusion for Long-Sequence Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12556)
*Fei Li,Qingyun Gao,Jianzhe Zhao,Guibing Guo,Beibei Kong,Lei Cheng,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种偏好漂移感知的子序列学习与分层上下文融合方法，用于长序列生成式推荐，兼顾准确率与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长序列生成式推荐分两类：全序列建模计算成本随长度增长且精度饱和、受噪声影响；目标感知上下文检索虽缩短输入，但受语义一致却偏好不一致的噪声及上下文不完整困扰。两者都忽略用户偏好动态变化与跨子序列依赖。

Method: 利用多维偏好漂移信息学习可微软子序列边界；以带软分配权重的线性注意力将子序列内物品聚合为偏好一致的表示；用交叉注意力捕捉近期交互与相关子序列上下文的依赖；最后通过门控融合机制自适应结合近期表示与全局子序列上下文。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在推荐准确率和计算效率上均持续优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 通过偏好漂移感知的子序列划分与分层上下文融合，有效缓解长序列噪声与效率问题，同时编码近期与长期偏好。

Abstract: Long-sequence generative recommendation methods autoregressively model the user's interaction sequence to generate the next-item representation. Existing methods generally fall into two categories: efficient full-sequence modeling and target-aware context retrieval. Our experiments reveal that as the sequence length increases, the former incurs steadily growing computational cost while its accuracy gains quickly saturate and even degrade due to noise; the latter, though shortening the input sequence, is susceptible to noise that is semantically consistent yet preference-inconsistent, as well as to incomplete contexts. Both paradigms ignore the dynamic changes of user preferences and the cross-subsequence dependencies when handling historical information, thereby limiting accuracy and efficiency. To address these issues, we propose a preference-drift-aware subsequence learning and hierarchical context fusion for long-sequence generative recommendation. Specifically, we learn differentiable soft subsequence boundaries using multidimensional preference-drift information and aggregate items within each subsequence into preference-coherent representations via linear attention with soft assignment weights, thereby circumventing the expense of full-sequence attention. A cross-attention mechanism is then employed to capture dependencies between recent interactions and relevant subsequence contexts, mitigating noise in learning recent-item representations. Finally, a gated fusion mechanism adaptively combines the recent-item representation with the global subsequence context, allowing the resulting target representation to encode both recent and long-term preferences. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing baselines in both recommendation accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [136] [Personalized and Trust-Aware Health Recommendation Policies for a Construction Workplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12679)
*Atefeh Mollabagher,Yogesh Gautam,Houtan Jebelli,Parinaz Naghizadeh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个动态模型，刻画建筑工人健康、信任与推荐系统之间的交互，并设计个性化健康推荐策略以平衡健康、生产力与信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑工人面临疲劳、热应激等职业风险，需及时、个性化的健康干预；但推荐效果依赖于工人信任与遵从度，需理解健康-信任-推荐的动态交互。

Method: 建立动态模型：健康随时间演化，信任受健康与推荐动态影响，信任又影响未来推荐的遵从度；用基于模型的短期控制和免模型强化学习求解推荐策略（触发阈值与推荐频率）。

Result: 刻画了基于健康的推荐触发阈值和推荐频率，并分析不同工人（健康与信任敏感度不同）如何调整推荐频率以平衡健康、生产力与信任。

Conclusion: 研究为建筑工作场所及更广泛场景下的个性化健康推荐策略设计提供洞见。

Abstract: Construction workers face workplace risks such as fatigue, heat stress, and other physically demanding conditions that can negatively affect their health and safety. Although monitoring these risks is important, timely and personalized health interventions are also needed to help prevent negative impacts on workers' well-being and productivity. To this end, in this paper, we propose a model to capture the interactions between a trust-aware health recommender system and workers who differ in health and trust sensitivity. Specifically, in our proposed dynamic model, worker health evolves over time, worker trust is affected by both health and recommendation dynamics, and trust in turn affects compliance with future recommendations. Given this model, we characterize the recommender policy, including a health-based recommendation triggering threshold and the recommendation frequency. We do so using both model-based short-horizon control and model-free reinforcement learning. We then investigate how recommendation frequencies are adjusted for different workers to balance their health, productivity, and trust. Our findings provide insight into the design of personalized health recommendation policies in construction workplaces and beyond.

</details>


### [137] [Learning the Lake: Reliable Experience for Adaptive Data Product Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12754)
*Yixi Zhou,Fan Zhang,Sikun Wang,Yingfan Xu,Haipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 SafeLake 框架，用演化发现记忆（EDM）在数据产品发现中安全地压缩重复搜索：熟悉度决定搜索多少，独立校准的证据决定往哪搜，在冻结转导协议下节省 49.5–82.7% 累积资产暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据产品发现即使工作负载重复访问相关产品和区域，仍需搜索整个数据湖；重复虽允许压缩搜索，但相似性无法为路由提供正当理由——漏掉任一资产都会使合取型产品失效。因此需要研究服务经验何时能安全减少此类工作。

Method: 提出演化发现记忆（EDM），在固定区域索引之上记录带来源标签的查询—产品—区域证据。SafeLake 将运营熟悉度（决定搜索预算）与独立校准的产品证据（决定搜索位置）分离。固定探针对比在 SafeLake 与仅熟悉度（Familiarity-only）之间保持自适应预算恒定。

Result: 在 TAT-QA 上，产品引导使 Product Recall 提升 0.072；ConvFinQA 未显示有效地图收益；HybridQA 敏感性分析中 Full R@100 更偏向仅熟悉度。仅轨迹、缺失与虚假反馈暴露了地图引导的边界，而范围审计一致性无法认证来源。在冻结转导协议下的干净确认反馈流中，形式化控制器节省 49.5–82.7% 的累积资产暴露。

Conclusion: 经验决定何时压缩，可靠证据决定在哪里压缩。

Abstract: Data-product discovery searches a full lake even when workloads revisit related products and regions. Repetition permits contracted search, but similarity cannot justify a route because one omitted asset invalidates a conjunctive product. We study when serving experience can safely reduce this work. Evolving Discovery Memory records source-labelled query--product--region evidence above a fixed regional index. SafeLake separates operational familiarity, which determines how much to search, from independently calibrated product evidence, which determines where to search. The fixed-probe comparison holds the adaptive budget constant between SafeLake and Familiarity-only. On TAT-QA, product steering raises Product Recall by 0.072; ConvFinQA shows no resolved map gain, while the HybridQA sensitivity favors Familiarity-only in Full R@100. Trace-only, missing, and false feedback expose boundaries on map steering, while scope-audit agreement cannot certify the source. Across clean confirmed-feedback streams under the frozen transductive protocol, the formal controller saves 49.5--82.7% of cumulative asset exposure. Experience determines when to contract; reliable evidence determines where to contract.

</details>


### [138] [A Historical Corpus Is Not a Historical System: Auditing Hindsight Leakage in Stateful Data Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12766)
*Yixi Zhou,Fan Zhang,Sikun Wang,Yingfan Xu,Haipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 point-in-time (PIT) discovery 形式化与 paired replay 协议，揭示离线回放中记忆（memory）未受约束导致 Future 视角高估检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线回放本应估计历史时点发现系统能检索到什么，但冻结语料却让交互记忆不受约束，造成评估偏差。

Method: 形式化 PIT 发现为历史状态 (D_t, θ_t, M_{<i})；提出 paired replay，仅改变记忆可用性；从 behavior-only traces 构建 PIT 与 full-stream Future 视角，并用 Temporal Violation Rate 审计。

Result: 跨 3 个 table-text 领域、2 种流设置、2 个 retriever、5 个 seed（216,000 行），Future 将 Asset Recall@100 抬高 2.62-5.24 点，12 个配对区间均不含零；behavior-only trace memory 下 PIT 低于无记忆 Stateless，Future 掩盖 32.7-48.4% 损害；正反馈缓存模拟中 PIT 比 Stateless 高 4.65-18.96 点，Future 再高 4.11-9.58 点；FreshStack 五话题上 Future 超 PIT 2.72 点 [1.75, 3.71]。

Conclusion: 历史评估必须随语料一起对记忆进行版本化与验证，否则会系统性高估发现系统性能。

Abstract: Offline replay should estimate what a discovery system could retrieve at a historical point, yet freezing the corpus leaves interaction memory unconstrained. We formalize point-in-time (PIT) discovery through historical state $(D_t, θ_t, M_{< i})$ and introduce a paired replay that changes only memory availability. The protocol constructs PIT and full-stream Future views from behavior-only traces and audits selected entries with a Temporal Violation Rate. Across three table-text domains, two stream regimes, two retrievers, and five seeds (216,000 rows), Future inflated Asset Recall@100 by 2.62-5.24 points; all 12 paired intervals excluded zero. With behavior-only trace memory, PIT underperformed the no-memory Stateless condition; Future masked 32.7-48.4% of that harm. For a simulated positive-feedback cache, PIT added 4.65-18.96 points over Stateless while Future added another 4.11-9.58 points. On five timestamped FreshStack topics, Future exceeded PIT by 2.72 points [1.75, 3.71]. Historical evaluation must version and validate memory with the corpus.

</details>


### [139] [MIMA: Multi-Interest Recommendation via Multi-Positive Exclusive Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12842)
*Xingyuan Mao,Alin Fan,Shichao Nie,Junfeng Zhang,Yan Xiao,Tao Luo,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MIMA，一种基于多正样本独占分配的多兴趣推荐框架，解决兴趣坍缩和跨兴趣分数不可比问题，实验与线上A/B测试均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多兴趣推荐用多个兴趣向量做细粒度候选匹配，但常出现兴趣坍缩（学到的兴趣趋于相似）。作者指出主因是普遍的单正样本范式：每条样本只有一个正样本，兴趣被独立优化，可能导致同一最佳匹配兴趣被反复朝不同正样本更新，而其他兴趣欠监督。此外，现有方法很少建模用户对各兴趣的激活强度，导致推理时不同兴趣通道的分数不可比。

Method: MIMA将同一请求内共现的物品分为一个正样本集合，用因果Transformer解码器生成互补兴趣，并通过匈牙利匹配将每个正样本独占分配给不同兴趣进行监督，使兴趣分化由训练目标本身产生而非依赖辅助正则。另设轻量路由模块估计用户-兴趣激活概率，以校准各兴趣通道的分数。

Result: 在三个公开数据集和一个工业数据集上，MIMA一致优于最先进基线；线上A/B测试取得显著业务收益。

Conclusion: 多正样本独占分配能有效缓解兴趣坍缩并增强兴趣区分度，激活概率校准提升了跨通道分数可比性，验证了MIMA的有效性与工业落地价值。

Abstract: Multi-interest recommendation represents each user with multiple interest vectors for fine-grained candidate matching, yet it often suffers from interest collapse, where the learned interests converge to similar representations. We highlight the prevailing single-positive paradigm as one important factor behind this issue. Since each instance provides only one positive item, intents are optimized independently, potentially causing the same best-matching interest to be repeatedly updated toward different positives while leaving the others under-supervised. Moreover, existing methods rarely model how strongly a user activates each interest, leaving scores from different interest channels incomparable at inference. To address these problems, we propose MIMA, a Multi-Interest recommendation framework built on Multi-positive exclusive Assignment. MIMA groups items co-occurring within the same request into a positive set, generates complementary interests with a causal Transformer decoder, and exclusively assigns each positive to supervise a distinct interest via Hungarian matching, so that interest differentiation emerges from the training objective itself rather than auxiliary regularization. A lightweight routing module further estimates user-interest activation probabilities to calibrate scores across interest channels. Experiments on three public datasets and an industrial dataset show that MIMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, and an online A/B test yields significant business gains.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [A Case-Bundle Operating Model for Coding Agents in OpenFOAM-Based CFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11941)
*Ke Xiao,Han Li,Teng Zhang,Yangchen Xu,Runze Mao,Zhi X. Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"case-bundle"运行模型，含Build与Replay两种模式，在OpenFOAM-7 interFoam喷射器设计筛选中验证，可复用自动化执行140个STL几何变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用编码智能体能搭建CFD算例、运行求解器并管理远程任务，但可审查、可复用的工作还需持久化的工程上下文与证据支撑。

Method: 采用case-bundle运行模型，分Build（工程审查下的智能体辅助算例开发）与Replay（将已审查算例应用于新变体）两模式。用GPT-5.5在Codex中开发算例包，内含仿真配置、几何处理与网格流程、远程执行脚本、后处理代码及审查记录；再以Pi编码智能体为运行时、搭配四种不同LLM后端做Replay。

Result: 在远程HPC上回放算例包，执行并后处理140个STL几何变体；另一组Replay使用四种LLM后端全部成功并产出经核验的结果，各次运行的工具使用与token消耗存在差异。

Conclusion: 经审查的算例包可支撑有边界的可复用自动化，并使常规执行与工程判断形成明确分工。

Abstract: General-purpose coding agents can set up computational fluid dynamics (CFD) cases, execute solvers, and manage remote jobs. Reviewable and reusable work additionally depends on persistent engineering context and evidence. We present a case-bundle operating model with two modes. Build supports agent-assisted case development under engineering review. Replay applies a reviewed case to new variants. We used this model in an OpenFOAM-7 interFoam study for screening injector designs. GPT-5.5 in Codex helped develop a case bundle containing the simulation configuration, geometry-processing and meshing procedures, remote-execution scripts, post-processing code, and review records. The bundle was replayed to execute and post-process 140 Stereolithography (STL) geometry variants on a remote high-performance computing system. A separate replay exercise used the Pi coding agent as the runtime with four different LLM backends. All four runs succeeded and produced verified results. Tool use and token consumption varied across runs. The results show how reviewed case bundles can support bounded, reusable automation with distinct roles for routine execution and engineering judgment.

</details>


### [141] [Is Bash All You Need? An Empirical Study of Tool Interfaces for Enterprise Digital Worker Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11999)
*Hazel Mak,Susheel Suresh,Sahil Bhatnagar,Barry Wang,Chhaya Methani,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在TheAgentCompany和APEX-Agents上比较五种工具接口，纯bash在得分和token效率上均优于typed tools，为通用shell在企业任务中的作用提供证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业任务涉及跨应用/服务操作、与同事协作和专业分析，而现有shell-based agent多在coding场景验证。作者想探究通用shell在企业任务上是否也能胜过专用工具接口。

Method: 在TheAgentCompany和APEX-Agents两个基准上，使用Opus-4.8和GPT-5.5，比较五种接口：typed tools、typed tools+bash、bash alone、带持久化agent合成工具的bash、programmatic tool calling（PTC，仅限调用typed工具目录）。

Result: 纯bash在TheAgentCompany上提升21.8-24.5 pp，在APEX-Agents上提升4.8-7.4 pp，同时减少19-72% token；添加typed tools或持久工具合成无显著增益；PTC token少于直接typed调用且性能相近，但整体在质量和成本效率上均不如纯bash。

Conclusion: 当可隔离任意执行时推荐纯bash；当安全或合规要求固定工具目录时推荐PTC。

Abstract: In this study, we examine whether a general shell can outperform specialized tools on enterprise tasks. Shell-based agents have shown strong results in coding, but enterprise work also involves moving between applications and services, coordinating with coworkers, and performing professional analysis. We compare five tool interfaces on TheAgentCompany and APEX-Agents using Opus-4.8 and GPT-5.5: typed tools, typed tools plus bash, bash alone, bash with persistent agent-synthesized tools, and programmatic tool calling (PTC), which runs programs whose actions are restricted to a typed tool catalog. Bash alone outperforms typed tools on both benchmarks, improving score by 21.8-24.5 pp on TheAgentCompany and 4.8-7.4 pp on APEX-Agents while using 19-72% fewer total tokens. Adding typed tools or persistent tool synthesis to bash produces no detectable pooled score gain. PTC uses fewer tokens than direct typed calls with broadly similar task performance, but generally underperforms bash alone in both quality and cost efficiency. For enterprise practitioners, these results favor bash alone when arbitrary execution can be isolated and PTC when security or compliance policies require a fixed tool catalog.

</details>


### [142] [Investigating Developer-Reported Software Security Testing Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12008)
*Md Erfan,Ahmed Ryan,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Stack Overflow上17,743个与软件安全测试(SST)相关的问题，手动标注582个问题，构建了8类31子类的挑战分类法，揭示开发者面临的核心挑战远超漏洞检测本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件安全测试对识别漏洞至关重要，但开发者在工具选择、环境配置、扫描结果解读、认证流程测试及漏洞修复等方面面临实际困难，这些开发者报告的真实挑战尚未被系统性地刻画。

Method: 从Stack Overflow用SST相关关键词收集17,743个问题，经手动标注识别出582个SST相关question，构建8类别31子类的分类法；随后进行prevalence、difficulty、correlation、temporal trend、co-occurrence及held-out stability分析。

Result: 开发者高频讨论安全发现解读与可靠性、测试指导与工具选择、认证授权测试、工具集成与自动化；验证类挑战需更多技术上下文和更长解决时间；安全发现解读与修复可操作性呈上升趋势；假阳性常与可解释性共现，集成问题常与工具建议和文档资源共现。

Conclusion: 开发者报告的SST挑战已超出漏洞检测范畴，涵盖工作流、配置、解读和修复等方面。这些发现可帮助研究者、从业者、教育者和工具提供方改进SST可用性、文档、结果解读与修复支持。

Abstract: Software security testing (SST) is essential for identifying vulnerabilities and improving software security, but developers often face practical challenges when selecting tools, configuring test environments, interpreting scanner outputs, testing authentication workflows, and acting on reported vulnerabilities. This study empirically characterizes developer-reported SST challenges in Stack Overflow (SO) discussions and examines their prevalence, difficulty, temporal evolution, co-occurrence, and taxonomy stability. We analyze 17,743 SO questions collected using SST-related keywords. Through manual labeling, we identify 582 SST-related questions and construct a taxonomy of 8 categories and 31 subcategories. We then analyze these challenges using prevalence, difficulty, correlation, temporal trend, co-occurrence, and held-out stability analyses.
